- **FE Explorer (Playwright MCP)**: Explores the running frontend through Playwright MCP, maps pages and user journeys, and records locator and flakiness risks plus prioritized FE test cases in .ai-outputs/. - **FE Test Implementer**: Implements Playwright UI tests for the FE tasks of a test plan, following the repository's conventions, and runs them before handing back. - **fe-a11y-auditor**: Audits clinozy-fe accessibility across whole pages — runs the axe-core Playwright lane (tests/e2e/accessibility.e2e.test.ts) when clinozy-be is up, otherwise sweeps components statically for ARIA/focus/label violations per web-design-guidelines. - **fe-agent**: 1. **页面开发**：根据 UI 原型和 API 文档开发前端页面 2. **组件开发**：编写可复用的 React 组件 3. **状态管理**：实现客户端状态（Zustand）和服务端状态（React Query） 4. **API 对接**：与后端 API 联调，处理请求/响应/错误 5. **Electron 适配**：Web 代码适配桌面端（窗口管理、本地文件、系统通知） 6. **性能优化**：懒加载、虚拟列表、bundle 优化 7. **响应式适配**：Web 端支持不同屏幕尺寸 ([meizhiyuannnnnn/Twisty-Hope](https://github.com/meizhiyuannnnnn/Twisty-Hope/blob/HEAD/agents/fe-agent.md) · ★1)

- **feature-builder**: MAKER agent — implement features for GeoChat (Next.js App Router + Supabase + MapLibre). Use when implementing a feature from a plan/spec. MUST NOT self-review. ([Quanghuy128/geochat](https://github.com/Quanghuy128/geochat/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-builder.md))
- **feature-builder**: Builds Pakwone's own implementation of a feature from the clean-room spec. Use after spec-writer, for the target product the user names. ([quantumnurobot/rea](https://github.com/quantumnurobot/rea/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-builder.md))
- **feature-builder**: Builds one approved feature in the iPropy CRM until its locked checks pass, wires it into the real app, and proves it in a browser. Launched by the build-feature skill only, after Rishabh has approved and locked the checks. ([RishabhSinghla/ipropy-crm](https://github.com/RishabhSinghla/ipropy-crm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/feature-builder.md))
